# Skills · I · part 20

851 entries, invariant-spec-tests to investor-pitch-script. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **invariant-spec-tests**: How to write a check that proves something — a test is a SPEC: the generator expressed for this implementation. Covers the spec discipline (one test per component or caveat, name = property, annotation = claim, specs grow while defects stay flat) and the assertion rules — smokes, probes, matrix rows, regression tests. Complements drive-pty (how to DRIVE) and the invariants skill (which owns the generator header form). Use when authoring or reviewing any test, smoke, harness probe, or verification section of a report. Every rule here was bought by a real failure. ([infinite-system/ivue](https://github.com/infinite-system/ivue/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/invariant-spec-tests) · ★12)
- **invariant-test-review**: Review tests to ensure they actually prove the claimed invariant, especially state-machine, simulation, oracle, and regression tests where extra setup, missing negative paths, or order-sensitive comparisons can hide the real signal ([ahrav/Gossip-rs](https://github.com/ahrav/Gossip-rs/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/invariant-test-review) · ★1)
- **invariant-test-review**: Use when writing or reviewing state-machine tests, simulation tests, oracle tests, or regression tests to verify they actually prove the claimed invariant. Catches hidden weaknesses like missing negative paths and order-sensitive comparisons. ([ahrav/Gossip-rs](https://github.com/ahrav/Gossip-rs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/invariant-test-review) · ★1)
- **invariant-writer**: Extract and document all system invariants, properties, and constraints from a smart contract codebase. Uses dual-mode analysis: 'What Should Happen' (positive specification from specs/standards) and 'What Must Never Happen' (adversarial multi-call attack sequences). Produces language-agnostic invariants consumed by fuzzing and formal verification tools. Use when preparing invariant suites, writing property specifications, or before fuzzing campaigns. ([calc1f4r/Horus](https://github.com/calc1f4r/Horus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/invariant-writer) · 2 copies)
